# EfficientNetV2 export

This directory contains the export scripts for the
[EfficientNetV2-s](https://docs.pytorch.org/vision/main/models/efficientnetv2.html)
classification model.

## How to run

For reproducible results this repository uses [`uv`](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/)
to manage dependencies. To install dependencies run:

```bash
uv sync
```

The Jupyter notebooks contain the code to export the models to Executorch
format.

## Profiling results

Below are the profiling results obtained according to the
[instructions](https://gl.swmansion.com/swm-ai/ai_knowledge_base/-/wikis/INTERNAL-DOCS/HOW-TO/PROFILE_EXPORTED_MODELS)
for the exported models.
